# American options with daily exercise, puts and calls: Stage A validation

Not part of the paper. `experiments/tournament.py --part amd_check / amd_check_ref / amd_random / amd_random_ref`, `experiments/american_daily_report.py`.

**Labels.** Design D2 hybrid (`MultiConfig()`): average of the out-of-sample and in-sample labels for price, Δ, Γ, Θ, Vega, Rho, Phi; out-of-sample for Volga, Vanna, ∂Δ/∂r, ∂Δ/∂d; 100,000 main + 100,000 pilot paths per run; exercise every business day, J = round(252 T) dates. Calls: payoff max(S − K, 0), European call as control variate, exact European label at d0 ≤ 0 ≤ r0, exercise-region values S0 − K, Δ = +1. Two variants, paired seeds: **D2-daily** (value function at t1)**, **D2-daily-vf50** (value function at 1/50 year). The second (`MultiConfig(vf_time=0.02)`) takes the value-function label at the exercise date nearest 1/50 year (the 5th daily date) instead of t1, with the exercise rule applied at the dates before it; at 50 dates a year it is the current label exactly.

**References.** Crank–Nicolson PDE (`reference.label_reference`): *American* = exercise at every time step, Richardson-extrapolated over 20 and 40 steps a day (continuous exercise, the target); *daily Bermudan* = exercise on the labels' dates (10 steps a day). Greeks in σ, r, d by bumping.

## Time and memory

| kind | T | labels | seconds per label (mean, by variant) | p95 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| put | 0.5 | 1800 | D2-daily 43, D2-daily-vf50 42 | 77 |
| put | 1 | 3400 | D2-daily 74, D2-daily-vf50 72 | 159 |
| put | 2 | 1800 | D2-daily 162, D2-daily-vf50 157 | 253 |
| call | 0.5 | 1800 | D2-daily 49, D2-daily-vf50 48 | 92 |
| call | 1 | 3400 | D2-daily 72, D2-daily-vf50 70 | 145 |
| call | 2 | 1800 | D2-daily 175, D2-daily-vf50 169 | 287 |

Peak memory (process RSS, maximum over the tasks running T = 2 labels): 681 MB. PDE reference per option: 27 s mean, 66 s max.

## Check sets: summary per Greek (default label)

35 put options (the production check's) and 35 call options (T 0.5/1/2 × σ 10/20/40 % × K 36/40/44 at r = 6 %, d = 4 %, plus r = 0, d = 4 %; r = 6 %, d = 0 (exact European); r = 6 %, d = 2 %), 100 labels each, seeds `job_seed(8010, {id}, rep)`. Cells: options with t > 3 / t < −3 · median signed relative bias · mean t · scaled bias Σ bias / Σ |ref| (t) · median noise of one label.

## 1,000 random points per type (one label each)

Points 20,000–20,999 (puts) and 21,000–21,999 (calls) of the scrambled Sobol sequence of the tournament / Stage 2 (S0/K 0.85–1.20, σ 10–40 %, T 0.25–2, d 0–4 %, r 0–6 % with 2 % at 0 and 25 % in (0, 3 %)); seeds `job_seed(8011, {id}, 0)`. Points with exact labels (no early exercise, or exercise at t0 in label and reference) left out. Bias = mean of (label − PDE)/|PDE| over the points with |PDE| ≥ 1 % of the largest (± se, t); scaled = Σ (label − PDE) / Σ |PDE| over all points (t); typical error = median |relative error|.
